Use when stress-testing a plan, pressure-testing a strategy, challenging assumptions, or before an executive review. ([72olabs/holler-roles](https://github.com/72olabs/holler-roles/tree/HEAD/kits/pm-skills/skills/strategy-red-team))
- **strategy-red-team**: Adversarial review of strategy work. Runs pre-mortems, assumption ledgers, failure-mode analysis, competitive attack simulation and counterargument generation against a brief, an insight, a positioning, an architecture recommendation, a loyalty model or a pitch narrative. Use at the end of every strategic loop, not only before a pitch. Trigger on "red team", "attack this", "pre-mortem", "devil's advocate", "stress test this", "where does this break", "what would a competitor do", "poke holes", "am I wrong about this", or automatically after any strategic recommendation is drafted. ([alifarrag-sudo/claude-skills](https://github.com/alifarrag-sudo/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/strategy-red-team))
- **strategy-red-team**: 对 PRD、路线图或战略进行红队攻击,在现实打脸之前先攻击其中的关键假设。先钢人化再攻击每一个论断,按 影响 × 可能性 × 测试成本 对失效模式排序,并为每一项返回成本最低的测试和终止标准。适用于压力测试计划、检验战略、挑战假设,或为高管评审准备文档。 ([Choi-Keith/skill-arsenal-ultra](https://github.com/Choi-Keith/skill-arsenal-ultra/tree/HEAD/plugins/pm-skills/pm-execution/skills/strategy-red-team))
- **strategy-red-team**: Stress-test major DAY AGENCY strategies, campaigns, offers and growth plans before execution. Use to challenge assumptions, identify hidden dependencies and propose falsification tests. ([Diarymedia13-web/Diarymedia](https://github.com/Diarymedia13-web/Diarymedia/tree/HEAD/DAY-AGENCY-SKILLS/V3/strategy-red-team))
- **strategy-red-team**: Adversarially stress-test an existing product strategy: surface its hidden load-bearing assumptions, steelman then attack each, rank failure modes by impact × likelihood × cheapness-to-test, and return a pre-mortem "why this fails" plus the falsifying test and kill criterion for each. Use when pressure-testing a strategy, challenging assumptions, running a pre-mortem, or preparing a strategy doc for executive review. - **strategy-red-team**: Red-team a PRD, roadmap, or strategy by attacking its load-bearing assumptions before reality does. Steelmans then attacks each claim, ranks failure modes by impact × likelihood × cheapness-to-test, and returns the cheapest test and kill criteria for each. Use when pressure-testing a strategy or roadmap, challenging the assumptions a plan rests on, or preparing a doc for executive review. For launch-readiness risk triage (what could go wrong at go-live), use pre-mortem. To build a strategy, or to test whether a document is a strategy at all (diagnosis, guiding policy, coherent actions), use s… ([Liohtml/ultimate-skills](https://github.com/Liohtml/ultimate-skills/tree/HEAD/product/skills/strategy-red-team))
- **strategy-red-team**: Red-team a bid, business case, roadmap or strategy by attacking its load-bearing assumptions before reality does. Steelmans each claim, then attacks it, ranks failure modes by impact, likelihood and cheapness-to-test, and returns the cheapest test and kill criteria for each. A structured challenge to received assumptions, not a longer risk list. Use when stress-testing a bid or a bid/no-bid decision, pressure-testing your own strategy, challenging a business case, or preparing a plan for board or executive review. - **strategy-red-team**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "strategy red team". Red-team a PRD, roadmap, or strategy by attacking its load-bearing assumptions before reality does. ([n2g7/agent-skills](https://github.com/n2g7/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/strategy-red-team))
- **strategy-red-team**: Use when asked to red-team, stress-test or pressure-test a plan, PRD, roadmap, launch, pricing or strategy, even one already chosen; to poke holes in it or ask what kills it; or to get it ready for an exec or investor review.
